function calcOp(rk,ck,hols){
  var ro=cWD[rk]||WD["108"];
  var cd=ro.calendars[ck]||ro.calendars["cal1"];
  var calHols = (cd.holidays && (wt === "6day" || !wt)) ? cd.holidays : hols;
  var res=[],sw=0,sh=0,so=0,su=0,sw2=0;
  for(var m=0;m<12;m++){
    var dim=MD[m];
    var tmin=cd.temp_min[m]||0,tmax=cd.temp_max[m]||0,rain=cd.rain[m]||0,snow=cd.snow[m]||0,wind=cd.wind[m]||0;
    var wd2=Math.round((tmin+tmax+rain+snow+wind)*10)/10;
    var hol=calHols[m]!==undefined?Number(calHols[m]):H6[m];
    var ov=Math.round(wd2*hol/31);
    var un=Math.ceil(wd2+hol-ov);
    var wk=dim-un;var rt=wk/dim;
    res.push({month:m+1,dim:dim,tmin:tmin,tmax:tmax,rain:rain,snow:snow,wind:wind,wd:wd2,hol:hol,ov:ov,un:un,wk:wk,rt:rt});
    sw+=wd2;sh+=hol;so+=ov;su+=un;sw2+=wk;
  }
  return{monthly:res,totals:{dim:365,wd:Math.round(sw*10)/10,hol:sh,ov:Math.round(so*10)/10,un:su,wk:sw2,yr:sw2/365}};
}
function renderOpTable(){
  var res=calcOp(reg,cal,cHol);
  var tb=document.getElementById("opTbody"),tf=document.getElementById("opTfoot");
  if(!tb)return;
  var h="";
  res.monthly.forEach(function(r,i){
    h+="<tr>"+
      '<td class="tc" style="font-weight:600">'+r.month+'월</td>'+
      '<td class="tr" style="color:#64748b">'+r.dim+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr ed" contenteditable="true" data-m="'+i+'" data-f="temp_min">'+r.tmin+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr ed" contenteditable="true" data-m="'+i+'" data-f="temp_max">'+r.tmax+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr ed" contenteditable="true" data-m="'+i+'" data-f="rain">'+r.rain+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr ed" contenteditable="true" data-m="'+i+'" data-f="snow">'+r.snow+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr ed" contenteditable="true" data-m="'+i+'" data-f="wind">'+r.wind+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr c-bh" style="font-weight:600">'+r.wd+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr edh" contenteditable="true" data-m="'+i+'">'+r.hol+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr" style="color:#64748b">'+r.ov+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr vr">'+r.un+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr vg">'+r.wk+'</td>'+
      '<td class="tr vb">'+(r.rt*100).toFixed(1)+'%</td>'+
      "</tr>";
  });
  tb.innerHTML=h;
  var t=res.totals;
  tf.innerHTML='<tr style="background:#f1f5f9;font-weight:700">'+
    '<td class="tc">합계/평균</td><td class="tr">365</td>'+
    '<td colspan="5" class="tc" style="font-size:0.73rem;color:#64748b">기상 요인별 연간 비작업일수</td>'+
    '<td class="tr" style="color:#1d4ed8">'+t.wd+'</td><td class="tr">'+t.hol+'</td><td class="tr">'+t.ov+'</td>'+
    '<td class="tr vr">'+t.un+'</td><td class="tr vg">'+t.wk+'</td>'+
    '<td class="tr" style="color:#1d4ed8;font-size:1rem">'+(t.yr*100).toFixed(1)+'%</td></tr>';
  document.querySelectorAll(".ed").forEach(function(c){
    c.addEventListener("blur",function(e){
      var mi=parseInt(e.target.dataset.m),f=e.target.dataset.f;
      var v=parseFloat(e.target.innerText)||0;
      if(cWD[reg]&&cWD[reg].calendars[cal])cWD[reg].calendars[cal][f][mi]=v;
      calcAndRender();
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".edh").forEach(function(c){
    c.addEventListener("blur",function(e){
      var mi=parseInt(e.target.dataset.m);
      cHol[mi]=parseFloat(e.target.innerText)||0;
      calcAndRender();
    });
  });
}
function renderChart(){
  var res=calcOp(reg,cal,cHol);
  var cc=document.getElementById("chartC");if(!cc)return;
  var h="";
  res.monthly.forEach(function(r){
    var p=(r.rt*100).toFixed(1),hp=r.rt*100;
    var bg=hp>=70?"linear-gradient(to top,#1d4ed8,#60a5fa)":hp>=50?"linear-gradient(to top,#d97706,#fbbf24)":"linear-gradient(to top,#b91c1c,#f87171)";
    h+='<div class="bw"><div class="bv">'+p+'%</div><div class="bar" style="height:'+(hp*0.85)+'%;background:'+bg+'"><span class="bl">'+r.month+'월</span></div></div>';
  });
  cc.innerHTML=h;
}
function calcAndRender(){renderOpTable();renderChart();if(aTab==="bal")renderWorkTable();else if(aTab==="rpt")renderRpt();}
function getVal(id,def){var e=document.getElementById(id);return e?e.value:def;}
function getCalRates(){
  return{none:1.0,cal1:calcOp(reg,"cal1",cHol).totals.yr,cal2:calcOp(reg,"cal2",cHol).totals.yr,cal3:calcOp(reg,"cal3",cHol).totals.yr,cal4:calcOp(reg,"cal4",cHol).totals.yr,cal5:calcOp(reg,"cal5",cHol).totals.yr,cal6:calcOp(reg,"cal6",cHol).totals.yr};
}
function updateWorkTotals(rates){
  rates = rates || getCalRates();
  var totalCD = 0;
  workItems.forEach(function(item){
    if (item.excludeFromSum) return;
    var r = rates[item.cal] || 1.0;
    var cd = item.customCD !== undefined ? item.customCD : (item.wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(item.wd / r) : 0);
    totalCD += cd;
  });
  var tf = document.getElementById("balTfoot");
  if (tf) {
    tf.innerHTML = '<tr class="tot" style="background:#fff;color:#0f172a;font-weight:800;font-size:0.95rem;border-top:2px solid #0f172a">' +
      '<td colspan="6" class="tc" style="padding:12px;color:#0f172a">전체 공사기간 최종 산출 합계</td>' +
      '<td class="tr" style="color:#0f172a;font-size:1.1rem;padding:12px;font-weight:900">' + totalCD.toLocaleString() + ' 일</td>' +
      '<td class="tr" style="color:#0f172a;font-size:1.1rem;padding:12px;font-weight:900">약 ' + (totalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1) + ' 개월</td>' +
      '<td colspan="2"></td></tr>';
  }
}

function renderWorkTable(){
  var tb = document.getElementById("balTbody");
  if (!tb) return;
  var rates = getCalRates();
  
  var catGroups = [];
  var currentCat = null;
  workItems.forEach(function(item, idx){
    if (!currentCat || currentCat.cat !== item.cat) {
      currentCat = { cat: item.cat, startIndex: idx, count: 1, items: [item] };
      catGroups.push(currentCat);
    } else {
      currentCat.count++;
      currentCat.items.push(item);
    }
  });

  var html = "";
  var globalIdx = 0;

  catGroups.forEach(function(group){
    var catSubTotalCD = 0;
    var lastItemIdxInGroup = group.startIndex + group.count - 1;
    var safeCatForJs = group.cat.replace(/'/g, "\\'").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');

    group.items.forEach(function(item, itemIdx){
      var i = globalIdx++;
      var r = rates[item.cal] || 1.0;
      var cd = item.customCD !== undefined ? item.customCD : (item.wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(item.wd / r) : 0);
      if (!item.excludeFromSum) {
        catSubTotalCD += cd;
      }
      var mo = (cd / 30.5).toFixed(1);
      var opts = CAL_OPTS.map(function(o){
        return '<option value="' + o.v + '"' + (item.cal === o.v ? ' selected' : '') + '>' + o.t + '</option>';
      }).join("");
      var safeCat = (item.cat || "").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
      var safeSubCat = (item.subCat || "").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');
      var safeNote = (item.note || "").replace(/"/g, '&quot;');

      html += '<tr data-i="' + i + '" draggable="true" class="work-row"' + (item.excludeFromSum ? ' style="background:#f8fafc;opacity:0.85"' : '') + '>';
      
      if (itemIdx === 0) {
        html += '<td rowspan="' + group.count + '" style="font-weight:700;background:#f8fafc;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center;border-right:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px 6px">' +
          '<input type="text" class="work-cat" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + safeCat + '" style="width:100%;font-weight:700;text-align:center;border:none;background:transparent;font-size:0.85rem;font-family:inherit;color:#0f172a" placeholder="대공종">' +
          '<div style="margin-top:6px"><button onclick="addSubWorkItem(\'' + safeCatForJs + '\', ' + lastItemIdxInGroup + ')" style="background:#eff6ff;color:#2563eb;border:1px solid #bfdbfe;border-radius:4px;padding:4px 8px;font-size:0.75rem;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;font-family:inherit;white-space:nowrap;box-shadow:0 1px 2px rgba(0,0,0,0.05)" title="이 대공종 내에 새 소공종 추가">+ 소공종 추가</button></div></td>';
      }

      html += '<td style="padding:4px 6px"><input type="text" class="work-subcat" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + safeSubCat + '" style="width:100%;min-width:110px;border:none;background:transparent;font-size:0.82rem;font-family:inherit;color:#1e293b;border-bottom:1px dashed #cbd5e1" placeholder="소공종 입력"></td>';
      html += '<td class="tc" style="padding:4px 2px"><input type="number" min="0" step="1" class="work-floors" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + (item.floors !== undefined ? item.floors : "") + '" style="width:56px;text-align:center;padding:4px 2px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:4px;font-size:0.85rem;font-family:inherit;background:#f8fafc"></td>';
      html += '<td class="tr c-yh" style="padding:4px 6px;white-space:nowrap"><input type="number" step="0.5" class="work-unitwd" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + (item.unitWD !== undefined ? item.unitWD : "") + '" style="width:68px;text-align:right;padding:4px 4px;border:1px solid #f59e0b;border-radius:4px;font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:600;font-family:inherit;background:#fffbeb"></td>';
      html += '<td class="tr c-yh" style="padding:4px 6px;white-space:nowrap"><input type="number" step="0.5" class="work-wd" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + item.wd + '" style="width:72px;text-align:right;padding:4px 4px;border:1px solid #f59e0b;border-radius:4px;font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:700;font-family:inherit;color:#1e293b;background:#fffbeb"></td>';
      html += '<td class="tc" style="padding:4px 4px"><select class="work-cal" data-i="' + i + '" style="font-size:0.75rem;background:white;color:#1e293b;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:4px;padding:3px 4px;font-family:inherit;width:110px">' + opts + '</select>' +
        '<div style="font-size:0.7rem;font-weight:700;color:#2563eb;margin-top:2px">' + (r * 100).toFixed(1) + '%</div></td>';
      html += '<td class="tr c-bh" style="padding:4px 6px;white-space:nowrap"><input type="number" step="1" class="work-cd" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + cd + '" style="width:76px;text-align:right;padding:4px 4px;border:1px solid #93c5fd;border-radius:4px;font-size:0.85rem;font-weight:800;font-family:inherit;color:' + (cd < 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#1e40af') + ';background:#eff6ff"> 일' +
        (item.excludeFromSum ? '<div style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#94a3b8;margin-top:2px;font-weight:600">(산입 제외)</div>' : '') + '</td>';
      html += '<td class="tr c-ph" style="font-weight:700;white-space:nowrap;padding:4px 6px;color:#5b21b6">' + mo + ' 개월</td>';
      html += '<td style="padding:4px 6px"><input type="text" class="work-note" data-i="' + i + '" value="' + safeNote + '" style="width:100%;min-width:120px;border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:4px;padding:3px 6px;font-size:0.78rem;font-family:inherit;color:#475569" placeholder=""></td>';
      html += '<td class="tc" style="width:125px;white-space:nowrap;padding:4px 2px">' +
        '<span class="drag-handle" title="드래그하여 순서 변경">⋮⋮</span>' +
        '<label style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;margin-right:4px;cursor:pointer" title="소계 및 총공기 합산 제외 여부"><input type="checkbox" class="work-excl" data-i="' + i + '" ' + (item.excludeFromSum ? 'checked' : '') + '> 제외</label>' +
        '<button onclick="moveWorkItem(' + i + ', -1)" style="background:none;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:3px;cursor:' + (i === 0 ? 'default;opacity:0.3' : 'pointer') + ';font-size:0.75rem;padding:2px 4px;margin-right:2px" title="위로" ' + (i === 0 ? 'disabled' : '') + '>▲</button>' +
        '<button onclick="moveWorkItem(' + i + ', 1)" style="background:none;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-radius:3px;cursor:' + (i === workItems.length - 1 ? 'default;opacity:0.3' : 'pointer') + ';font-size:0.75rem;padding:2px 4px;margin-right:4px" title="아래로" ' + (i === workItems.length - 1 ? 'disabled' : '') + '>▼</button>' +
        '<button onclick="removeWorkItem(' + i + ')" style="background:none;border:1px solid #fca5a5;border-radius:3px;cursor:pointer;color:#ef4444;font-size:0.9rem;padding:1px 5px;line-height:1.2" title="이 공종 삭제">×</button></td>';

      html += '</tr>';
    });

    html += '<tr style="background:#f1f5f9;font-weight:700;border-top:1px solid #cbd5e1;border-bottom:2px solid #cbd5e1">' +
      '<td colspan="6" class="tr" style="padding:6px 12px;color:#475569;font-size:0.8rem">[' + (group.cat || "").replace(/"/g, '&quot;') + '] 소계</td>' +
      '<td class="tr" style="color:#1e40af;font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:800;padding:6px 8px">' + catSubTotalCD + ' 일</td>' +
      '<td class="tr" style="color:#5b21b6;font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:800;padding:6px 8px">' + (catSubTotalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1) + ' 개월</td>' +
      '<td colspan="2"></td></tr>';
  });

  tb.innerHTML = html;
  updateWorkTotals(rates);

  document.querySelectorAll(".work-cat").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      var oldCat = workItems[i].cat;
      var newCat = e.target.value;
      workItems.forEach(function(item){ if(item.cat === oldCat) item.cat = newCat; });
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-subcat").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      workItems[parseInt(e.target.dataset.i)].subCat = e.target.value;
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-floors").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      workItems[i].floors = parseFloat(e.target.value) || "";
      if (workItems[i].floors && workItems[i].unitWD) {
        workItems[i].wd = Math.round(workItems[i].floors * workItems[i].unitWD * 10) / 10;
        delete workItems[i].customCD;
        var row = e.target.closest("tr");
        if (row) {
          var wdInp = row.querySelector(".work-wd");
          if (wdInp) wdInp.value = workItems[i].wd;
          var rates2 = getCalRates();
          var r = rates2[workItems[i].cal] || 1.0;
          var cd = workItems[i].wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(workItems[i].wd / r) : 0;
          var cdInp = row.querySelector(".work-cd");
          if (cdInp) cdInp.value = cd;
        }
      }
      updateWorkTotals();
    });
    inp.addEventListener("change", function(){ renderWorkTable(); });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-unitwd").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      workItems[i].unitWD = parseFloat(e.target.value) || "";
      if (workItems[i].floors && workItems[i].unitWD) {
        workItems[i].wd = Math.round(workItems[i].floors * workItems[i].unitWD * 10) / 10;
        delete workItems[i].customCD;
      } else if (workItems[i].unitWD && !workItems[i].floors) {
        workItems[i].wd = workItems[i].unitWD;
        delete workItems[i].customCD;
      }
      var row = e.target.closest("tr");
      if (row) {
        var wdInp = row.querySelector(".work-wd");
        if (wdInp) wdInp.value = workItems[i].wd;
        var rates2 = getCalRates();
        var r = rates2[workItems[i].cal] || 1.0;
        var cd = workItems[i].wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(workItems[i].wd / r) : 0;
        var cdInp = row.querySelector(".work-cd");
        if (cdInp) cdInp.value = cd;
      }
      updateWorkTotals();
    });
    inp.addEventListener("change", function(){ renderWorkTable(); });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-wd").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      workItems[i].wd = parseFloat(e.target.value) || 0;
      delete workItems[i].customCD;
      var row = e.target.closest("tr");
      if (row) {
        var rates2 = getCalRates();
        var r = rates2[workItems[i].cal] || 1.0;
        var cd = workItems[i].wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(workItems[i].wd / r) : 0;
        var cdInp = row.querySelector(".work-cd");
        if (cdInp) cdInp.value = cd;
      }
      updateWorkTotals();
    });
    inp.addEventListener("change", function(){ renderWorkTable(); });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-cal").forEach(function(sel){
    sel.addEventListener("change", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      workItems[i].cal = e.target.value;
      delete workItems[i].customCD;
      renderWorkTable();
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-cd").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      workItems[i].customCD = parseFloat(e.target.value);
      if (isNaN(workItems[i].customCD)) delete workItems[i].customCD;
      var rates2 = getCalRates();
      updateWorkTotals(rates2);
    });
    inp.addEventListener("change", function(e){
      renderWorkTable();
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-excl").forEach(function(chk){
    chk.addEventListener("change", function(e){
      var i = parseInt(e.target.dataset.i);
      workItems[i].excludeFromSum = e.target.checked;
      renderWorkTable();
    });
  });
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-note").forEach(function(inp){
    inp.addEventListener("input", function(e){
      workItems[parseInt(e.target.dataset.i)].note = e.target.value;
    });
  });

  // Drag & Drop Reordering Event Listeners
  var draggedIdx = null;
  document.querySelectorAll(".work-row").forEach(function(row){
    row.addEventListener("dragstart", function(e){
      draggedIdx = parseInt(this.dataset.i);
      this.classList.add("dragging");
      e.dataTransfer.effectAllowed = "move";
      e.dataTransfer.setData("text/plain", draggedIdx);
    });
    row.addEventListener("dragend", function(e){
      this.classList.remove("dragging");
      document.querySelectorAll(".work-row").forEach(function(r){ r.classList.remove("drag-over"); });
    });
    row.addEventListener("dragover", function(e){
      e.preventDefault();
      e.dataTransfer.dropEffect = "move";
      var targetIdx = parseInt(this.dataset.i);
      if (draggedIdx !== null && draggedIdx !== targetIdx) {
        this.classList.add("drag-over");
      }
    });
    row.addEventListener("dragleave", function(e){
      this.classList.remove("drag-over");
    });
    row.addEventListener("drop", function(e){
      e.preventDefault();
      this.classList.remove("drag-over");
      var targetIdx = parseInt(this.dataset.i);
      if (draggedIdx !== null && draggedIdx !== targetIdx) {
        var movedItem = workItems.splice(draggedIdx, 1)[0];
        workItems.splice(targetIdx, 0, movedItem);
        draggedIdx = null;
        renderWorkTable();
      }
    });
  });
}

function addWorkItem(){
  workItems.push({cat:"기타공사", subCat:"신규 공종", floors:"", unitWD:"", wd:0, cal:"cal1", customCD:0, note:""});
  renderWorkTable();
  setTimeout(function(){
    var ins = document.querySelectorAll(".work-subcat");
    if(ins.length) ins[ins.length-1].focus();
  }, 60);
}

function addSubWorkItem(catName, insertAfterIndex){
  var newItem = {
    cat: catName || "기타공사",
    subCat: "신규 소공종",
    floors: 1,
    unitWD: 1,
    wd: 1,
    cal: "cal3",
    note: ""
  };
  if (insertAfterIndex !== undefined && insertAfterIndex >= 0) {
    workItems.splice(insertAfterIndex + 1, 0, newItem);
  } else {
    workItems.push(newItem);
  }
  renderWorkTable();
  setTimeout(function(){
    var ins = document.querySelectorAll(".work-subcat");
    var targetIdx = insertAfterIndex !== undefined ? insertAfterIndex + 1 : ins.length - 1;
    if (ins[targetIdx]) ins[targetIdx].focus();
  }, 60);
}
function removeWorkItem(i){
  if(workItems.length<=1){alert("최소 1개의 공종이 필요합니다.");return;}
  workItems.splice(i,1);
  renderWorkTable();
}
function moveWorkItem(i, dir){
  if(i+dir < 0 || i+dir >= workItems.length) return;
  var temp = workItems[i];
  workItems[i] = workItems[i+dir];
  workItems[i+dir] = temp;
  renderWorkTable();
}
function renderRpt(){
  var rc=document.getElementById("rptContent");if(!rc)return;
  var pn=getVal("proj_name","(사업명 미입력)") || "(사업명 미입력)";
  var sa=getVal("site_area","") || "-";
  var ta=getVal("tot_area","") || "-";
  var blkv=getVal("blk","") || "-";
  var uv=getVal("units","") || "-";
  var bsv=getVal("bsmt","") || "-";
  var grv=getVal("grnd","") || "-";
  var rn=(cWD[reg]||WD["108"]).name;
  var c1=calcOp(reg,"cal1",cHol),c3=calcOp(reg,"cal3",cHol),c5=calcOp(reg,"cal5",cHol);
  var rates=getCalRates();
  var today=new Date().toLocaleDateString("ko-KR",{year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric"});

  var tr2="";
  ["cal1","cal2","cal3","cal4","cal5","cal6"].forEach(function(ck){
    var r=calcOp(reg,ck,cHol),inf=CINFO[ck];
    tr2+='<tr><td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;font-weight:500">'+inf.name+'</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;text-align:center">'+ck.toUpperCase()+'</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;color:#475569;font-size:0.75rem">'+inf.desc+'</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;text-align:right">'+r.totals.wd+'일</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;text-align:right">'+r.totals.hol+'일</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;text-align:right;color:#dc2626;font-weight:700">'+r.totals.un+'일</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px;text-align:right;color:#2563eb;font-weight:700">'+(r.totals.yr*100).toFixed(1)+'%</td></tr>';
  });

  // 공종별 산출 내역 - 현재 workItems 사용
  var catGroups=[];var curCat=null;
  workItems.forEach(function(item,idx){
    if(!curCat||curCat.cat!==item.cat){curCat={cat:item.cat,count:1,items:[item]};catGroups.push(curCat);}
    else{curCat.count++;curCat.items.push(item);}
  });
  var totalCD=0;var workRows="";
  catGroups.forEach(function(group){
    var groupSubCD=0;
    group.items.forEach(function(item,itemIdx){
      var r2=rates[item.cal]||1.0;
      var cd=item.customCD!==undefined?item.customCD:(item.wd>0?Math.ceil(item.wd/r2):0);
      if(!item.excludeFromSum){groupSubCD+=cd;totalCD+=cd;}
      var mo=(cd/30.5).toFixed(1);
      workRows+='<tr'+(item.excludeFromSum?' style="background:#f8fafc"':'')+'>'+
        (itemIdx===0?'<td rowspan="'+group.count+'" style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;font-weight:700;background:#f8fafc;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center">'+escHtml(group.cat)+'</td>':'')+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;font-weight:500">'+escHtml(item.subCat||"")+'</td>'+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;text-align:center;font-size:0.8rem">'+(item.floors||"")+'</td>'+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#d97706">'+item.wd+' 일</td>'+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#2563eb">'+(r2*100).toFixed(1)+'%</td>'+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;font-weight:800;color:'+(cd<0?'#dc2626':'#2563eb')+'">'+cd+' 일'+(item.excludeFromSum?'<br><span style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#94a3b8">(산입 제외)</span>':'')+' </td>'+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;text-align:right;color:#7c3aed;font-weight:700">'+mo+' 개월</td>'+
        '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:7px 10px;font-size:0.75rem;color:#475569">'+escHtml(item.note||"")+'</td>'+
        '</tr>';
    });
    workRows+='<tr style="background:#f1f5f9;font-weight:700">'+
      '<td colspan="5" style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:6px 10px;text-align:right;color:#475569">['+escHtml(group.cat)+'] 소계</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:6px 10px;text-align:right;color:#1e40af">'+groupSubCD+' 일</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:6px 10px;text-align:right;color:#5b21b6">'+(groupSubCD/30.5).toFixed(1)+' 개월</td>'+
      '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1"></td></tr>';
  });

  rc.innerHTML='<div class="card"><div class="card-body">'+
    // ── 헤더 ──
    '<div style="display:flex;justify-content:space-between;align-items:flex-start;border-bottom:2px solid #e2e8f0;padding-bottom:16px;margin-bottom:20px">'+
    '<div><span style="display:inline-block;padding:4px 10px;background:#dbeafe;color:#1e40af;border-radius:20px;font-size:0.72rem;font-weight:700;margin-bottom:8px">2026 적정공사 가이드라인 산출 보고서</span>'+
    '<h2 style="font-size:1.3rem;font-weight:800;color:#0f172a">['+escHtml(pn)+'] 시공공정계획 및 공사가동률 검토서</h2></div>'+
    '<div style="text-align:right;font-size:0.72rem;color:#64748b">기준: 2026년 국토부 가이드라인<br>적용지점: '+rn+'<br>작성일: '+today+'</div></div>'+
    // ── 프로젝트 기본 정보 ──
    '<div class="rst">0. 프로젝트 기본 정보</div>'+
    '<div style="display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:8px;margin-bottom:20px">'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:10px"><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px">사업명</div><div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:700;color:#0f172a">'+escHtml(pn)+'</div></div>'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:10px"><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px">대지면적</div><div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:700">'+escHtml(sa)+' ㎡</div></div>'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:10px"><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px">연면적</div><div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:700">'+escHtml(ta)+' ㎡</div></div>'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:10px"><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px">동 수</div><div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:700">'+escHtml(blkv)+' 개동</div></div>'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:10px"><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px">세대수</div><div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:700">'+escHtml(uv)+' 세대</div></div>'+
    '<div style="border:1px solid #e2e8f0;border-radius:6px;padding:10px"><div style="font-size:0.7rem;color:#64748b;font-weight:600;margin-bottom:4px">규모</div><div style="font-size:0.9rem;font-weight:700">지하 B'+escHtml(bsv)+'F / 지상 '+escHtml(grv)+'F</div></div>'+
    '</div>'+
    // ── 가동률 요약 카드 ──
    '<div class="rsg">'+
    '<div class="rsc"><div class="rsl">토목공사 가동률 (Cal 1)</div><div class="rsv" style="color:#2563eb">'+(c1.totals.yr*100).toFixed(1)+'%</div><div class="rss">연간 비작업일수: '+c1.totals.un+'일</div></div>'+
    '<div class="rsc"><div class="rsl">지상골조 가동률 (Cal 3)</div><div class="rsv" style="color:#059669">'+(c3.totals.yr*100).toFixed(1)+'%</div><div class="rss">연간 비작업일수: '+c3.totals.un+'일</div></div>'+
    '<div class="rsc"><div class="rsl">외부마감 가동률 (Cal 5)</div><div class="rsv" style="color:#7c3aed">'+(c5.totals.yr*100).toFixed(1)+'%</div><div class="rss">연간 비작업일수: '+c5.totals.un+'일</div></div>'+
    '</div>'+
    // ── 캘린더별 가동률 비교표 ──
    '<div class="rst">1. 공종별 작업조건 및 산출 가동률 비교</div>'+
    '<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:0.82rem;margin-bottom:20px">'+
    '<thead><tr style="background:#f1f5f9">'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">공종 구분</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">적용 캘린더</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">기상 비작업 기준</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;text-align:right">기상일수(a)</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;text-align:right">공휴일(b)</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;text-align:right">공사불능일</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;text-align:right">연간 가동률</th>'+
    '</tr></thead><tbody>'+tr2+'</tbody></table>'+
    // ── 공종별 공사기간 산출 내역 ──
    '<div class="rst">2. 공종별 개략 공사기간 산출 내역</div>'+
    '<table style="width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:0.82rem;margin-bottom:20px">'+
    '<thead><tr style="background:#f1f5f9">'+
    '<th colspan="2" style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">구 분</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">층수</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;background:#fefce8;color:#854d0e">순작업일수(W.D)</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">가동률</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;background:#eff6ff;color:#1e40af">달력일수(C.D)</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px;background:#f5f3ff;color:#5b21b6">산입공기</th>'+
    '<th style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:8px">비고</th>'+
    '</tr></thead>'+
    '<tbody>'+workRows+'</tbody>'+
    '<tfoot><tr style="background:#f1f5f9;font-weight:800">'+
    '<td colspan="5" style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:9px;text-align:center">전체 공사기간 최종 산출 합계</td>'+
    '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:9px;text-align:right;color:#0f172a;font-size:1rem">'+totalCD+' 일</td>'+
    '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1;padding:9px;text-align:right;color:#0f172a;font-size:1rem">약 '+(totalCD/30.5).toFixed(1)+' 개월</td>'+
    '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1"></td>'+
    '</tr></tfoot></table>'+
    // ── 참고사항 ──
    '<div class="gb"><strong>📌 참고사항 및 2026년 가이드라인 준수여부</strong><br>'+
    '• 2026년 국토교통부 적정 공사기간 확보를 위한 가이드라인의 최신 법정공휴일(69일) 및 기상 비작업 기준을 정확히 반영하였습니다.<br>'+
    '• 본 보고서는 현재 산출 탭에 입력된 공종별 수치를 실시간으로 반영하며, 저장 내역을 불러올 경우 해당 시점의 내용이 표시됩니다.'+
    '</div></div></div>';
}
function exportPDF(id) {
  if (id === undefined) {
    alert("산출 내역을 먼저 저장한 후, 저장 내역 목록에서 PDF 출력을 실행해 주세요.");
    return;
  }
  var list = getHistoryList();
  var rec = list.find(function(r){ return r.id === id; });
  if (!rec) {
    alert("해당 산출 내역을 찾을 수 없습니다.");
    return;
  }

  var pn, sa, ta, blkv, uv, bsv, grv, rn, wtLabel, today;
  var calRatesData, workItemsData;
  today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("ko-KR", {year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric"});

  pn = rec.projName; sa = rec.projInfo.siteArea || "-"; ta = rec.projInfo.totArea || "-";
  blkv = rec.projInfo.blk || "-"; uv = rec.projInfo.units || "-";
  bsv = rec.projInfo.bsmt || "-"; grv = rec.projInfo.grnd || "-";
  rn = rec.region; wtLabel = rec.workType;
  calRatesData = rec.calRates;
  workItemsData = rec.workItems;

  var calLabels = {cal1:"Cal 1. 토목공사",cal2:"Cal 2. 지하/골조(습식)",cal3:"Cal 3. 지상골조/외부(습식)",cal4:"Cal 4. 내부마감(습식)",cal5:"Cal 5. 외부(건식)",cal6:"Cal 6. 내부마감(건식)"};
  var calColors = {cal1:"#2563eb",cal2:"#059669",cal3:"#0891b2",cal4:"#7c3aed",cal5:"#c2410c",cal6:"#475569"};

  // 캘린더 가동률 행
  var rateRows = ["cal1","cal2","cal3","cal4","cal5","cal6"].map(function(ck) {
    var rv = calRatesData[ck] !== undefined ? calRatesData[ck] : "-";
    return '<tr>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;font-weight:500">' + calLabels[ck] + '</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:center;font-weight:800;color:' + calColors[ck] + ';font-size:1.05rem">' + rv + '%</td>'
      + '</tr>';
  }).join("");

  // 공종별 공사기간 행
  var totalCD = 0;
  var workRows = workItemsData.map(function(item, i) {
    var cd = Number(item.cd) || 0;
    totalCD += cd;
    return '<tr>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:center;color:#94a3b8;font-size:0.78rem">' + (i+1) + '</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;font-weight:500">' + item.name + '</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:center;font-size:0.8rem;color:#475569">' + (item.calName||item.cal) + '</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#2563eb">' + item.rate + '%</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#d97706">' + item.wd + ' 일</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:800;color:#2563eb">' + cd + ' 일</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#7c3aed">' + (cd/30.5).toFixed(1) + ' 개월</td>'
      + '</tr>';
  }).join("");
  var totalMonth = (totalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1);

  var html = '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="ko"><head>'
    + '<meta charset="UTF-8">'
    + '<title>' + pn + ' — 공종별 개략 공사기간 산출서</title>'
    + '<style>'
    + '@import url("https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/orioncactus/pretendard/dist/web/static/pretendard.css");'
    + '* { box-sizing:border-box; margin:0; padding:0; }'
    + 'body { font-family:"Pretendard","Apple SD Gothic Neo",sans-serif; font-size:10pt; color:#0f172a; background:#fff; }'
    + '.page { width:210mm; min-height:297mm; margin:0 auto; padding:16mm 18mm 14mm; }'
    + '.doc-badge { display:inline-block; background:#dbeafe; color:#1e40af; border-radius:20px; padding:3px 12px; font-size:7.5pt; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:8px; }'
    + '.doc-title { font-size:15pt; font-weight:800; color:#0f172a; letter-spacing:-0.02em; margin-bottom:4px; }'
    + '.doc-sub   { font-size:8.5pt; color:#64748b; margin-bottom:0; }'
    + '.doc-meta  { font-size:8pt; color:#64748b; text-align:right; }'
    + '.title-row { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-end; border-bottom:2.5px solid #0f172a; padding-bottom:12px; margin-bottom:18px; }'
    + '.section-title { font-size:9.5pt; font-weight:700; color:#0f172a; background:#f1f5f9; padding:6px 10px; border-left:3px solid #3b82f6; margin-bottom:10px; margin-top:18px; }'
    + '.info-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr; gap:8px; margin-bottom:4px; }'
    + '.info-cell { border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:5px; padding:8px 10px; }'
    + '.info-label { font-size:7pt; color:#64748b; font-weight:600; margin-bottom:3px; }'
    + '.info-value { font-size:9.5pt; font-weight:700; color:#0f172a; }'
    + 'table { width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:8.5pt; }'
    + 'th { background:#f8fafc; padding:8px 10px; font-weight:700; border:1px solid #cbd5e1; text-align:center; white-space:nowrap; }'
    + 'tbody tr:nth-child(even) td { background:#fafafa; }'
    + 'tfoot td { background:#f1f5f9; font-weight:800; border:1px solid #cbd5e1; }'
    + '.total-row td { padding:9px 10px; font-size:9.5pt; }'
    + '.stamp-area { margin-top:24px; display:flex; justify-content:flex-end; gap:0; }'
    + '.stamp-box { border:1px solid #cbd5e1; text-align:center; }'
    + '.stamp-hdr { background:#f1f5f9; padding:5px 20px; font-size:7.5pt; font-weight:700; border-bottom:1px solid #cbd5e1; }'
    + '.stamp-body { height:50px; width:80px; }'
    + '.footer-note { margin-top:20px; border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0; padding-top:10px; font-size:7pt; color:#94a3b8; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; }'
    + '@media print {'
    + '  body { -webkit-print-color-adjust:exact; print-color-adjust:exact; }'
    + '  .page { padding:10mm 14mm; }'
    + '  @page { size:A4 portrait; margin:0; }'
    + '}'
    + '</style></head><body>'
    + '<div class="page">'
    + '<div class="title-row">'
    + '<div>'
    + '<div class="doc-badge">2026 국토부 적정공사기간 가이드라인 기준</div>'
    + '<div class="doc-title">공종별 개략 공사기간 산출서</div>'
    + '<div class="doc-sub">[' + pn + ']&nbsp;&nbsp;시공공정계획 검토</div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="doc-meta">작성일: ' + today + '<br>적용지점: ' + rn + '<br>휴무조건: ' + wtLabel + '</div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="section-title">■ 프로젝트 기본 정보</div>'
    + '<div class="info-grid">'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">사업명</div><div class="info-value">' + pn + '</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">대지면적</div><div class="info-value">' + sa + ' ㎡</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">연면적</div><div class="info-value">' + ta + ' ㎡</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">동 수</div><div class="info-value">' + blkv + ' 개동</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">세대수</div><div class="info-value">' + uv + ' 세대</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">규모</div><div class="info-value">지하 B' + bsv + 'F / 지상 ' + grv + 'F</div></div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="section-title">■ 캘린더별 연간 공사가동률 요약</div>'
    + '<table>'
    + '<thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">공종 구분</th><th>연간 가동률</th></tr></thead>'
    + '<tbody>' + rateRows + '</tbody>'
    + '</table>'
    + '<div class="section-title">■ 공종별 개략 공사기간 산출 (가동률 적용)</div>'
    + '<table>'
    + '<thead><tr>'
    + '<th style="width:28px">#</th>'
    + '<th style="text-align:left">주요 공종 구분</th>'
    + '<th>적용 캘린더</th>'
    + '<th>작업 가동률</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#fefce8;color:#854d0e">순작업일수<br>(W.D 일)</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#eff6ff;color:#1e40af">달력일수<br>(C.D 일)</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#f5f3ff;color:#5b21b6">소요개월수<br>(개월)</th>'
    + '</tr></thead>'
    + '<tbody>' + workRows + '</tbody>'
    + '<tfoot><tr class="total-row">'
    + '<td colspan="5" style="text-align:center;padding:9px 10px">🏆&nbsp; 전체 공사기간 합계</td>'
    + '<td style="text-align:right;color:#f59e0b;font-size:11pt;padding:9px 10px">' + totalCD + ' 일</td>'
    + '<td style="text-align:right;color:#f59e0b;font-size:11pt;padding:9px 10px">약 ' + totalMonth + ' 개월</td>'
    + '</tr></tfoot>'
    + '</table>'
    + '<div class="stamp-area">'
    + '<div class="stamp-box"><div class="stamp-hdr">검 토</div><div class="stamp-body"></div></div>'
    + '<div class="stamp-box"><div class="stamp-hdr">확 인</div><div class="stamp-body"></div></div>'
    + '<div class="stamp-box"><div class="stamp-hdr">승 인</div><div class="stamp-body"></div></div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="footer-note">'
    + '<span>※ 본 산출서는 2026년 국토교통부 적정 공사기간 확보를 위한 가이드라인 기상 비작업 기준을 적용하여 산정한 것입니다.</span>'
    + '<span>작성: 공사가동률 산출 시스템</span>'
    + '</div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();}<\/script>'
    + '</body></html>';

  var w = window.open("", "_blank", "width=900,height=1100");
  if (!w) { alert("팝업이 차단되었습니다. 브라우저에서 팝업을 허용해 주세요."); return; }
  w.document.open();
  w.document.write(html);
  w.document.close();
}

// ─── 산출 저장 & 내역 조회 ───────────────────────────────────────────────
var STORAGE_KEY = "constructionCalcHistory";

function saveResult() {
  var pn = getVal("proj_name", "미입력");
  var regionName = (cWD[reg] || WD["108"]).name;
  var rates = getCalRates();
  var totalCD = 0;
  var itemsSnap = workItems.map(function(item) {
    var r = rates[item.cal] || 1.0;
    var cd = item.customCD !== undefined ? item.customCD : (item.wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(item.wd / r) : 0);
    if (!item.excludeFromSum) {
      totalCD += cd;
    }
    return {
      cat: item.cat || "",
      subCat: item.subCat || item.name || "",
      floors: item.floors !== undefined ? item.floors : "",
      unitWD: item.unitWD !== undefined ? item.unitWD : "",
      wd: item.wd,
      cal: item.cal,
      calName: (CAL_OPTS.find(function(o){return o.v===item.cal;})||{t:"전공종"}).t,
      cd: cd,
      rate: (r * 100).toFixed(1),
      note: item.note || "",
      excludeFromSum: !!item.excludeFromSum
    };
  });
  var calRatesSummary = {};
  ["cal1","cal2","cal3","cal4","cal5","cal6"].forEach(function(ck){
    calRatesSummary[ck] = (rates[ck] * 100).toFixed(1);
  });
  var record = {
    id: Date.now(),
    savedAt: new Date().toLocaleString("ko-KR"),
    projName: pn,
    region: regionName,
    workType: wt === "6day" ? "주 6일 근무" : "주 5일 근무",
    projInfo: {
      siteArea: getVal("site_area",""),
      totArea: getVal("tot_area",""),
      blk: getVal("blk",""),
      units: getVal("units",""),
      bsmt: getVal("bsmt",""),
      grnd: getVal("grnd","")
    },
    calRates: calRatesSummary,
    workItems: itemsSnap,
    totalCD: totalCD,
    totalMonth: (totalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1)
  };
  var list = getHistoryList();
  list.unshift(record);
  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)); } catch(e) {}
  loadHistoryList();
  var btn = document.getElementById("saveBtn");
  var orig = btn.innerHTML;
  btn.innerHTML = "✅ 저장 완료!";
  btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg,#10b981,#059669)";
  setTimeout(function(){
    btn.innerHTML = orig;
    btn.style.background = "";
  }, 1800);
}

function exportPDF(id) {
  if (id === undefined) {
    alert("산출 내역을 먼저 저장한 후, 저장 내역 목록에서 PDF 출력을 실행해 주세요.");
    return;
  }
  var list = getHistoryList();
  var rec = list.find(function(r){ return r.id === id; });
  if (!rec) {
    alert("해당 산출 내역을 찾을 수 없습니다.");
    return;
  }

  var pn, sa, ta, blkv, uv, bsv, grv, rn, wtLabel, today;
  var calRatesData, workItemsData;
  today = new Date().toLocaleDateString("ko-KR", {year:"numeric",month:"long",day:"numeric"});

  pn = rec.projName; sa = rec.projInfo.siteArea || "-"; ta = rec.projInfo.totArea || "-";
  blkv = rec.projInfo.blk || "-"; uv = rec.projInfo.units || "-";
  bsv = rec.projInfo.bsmt || "-"; grv = rec.projInfo.grnd || "-";
  rn = rec.region; wtLabel = rec.workType;
  calRatesData = rec.calRates;
  workItemsData = rec.workItems;

  var calLabels = {cal1:"Cal 1. 토목공사",cal2:"Cal 2. 지하/골조(습식)",cal3:"Cal 3. 지상골조/외부(습식)",cal4:"Cal 4. 내부마감(습식)",cal5:"Cal 5. 외부(건식)",cal6:"Cal 6. 내부마감(건식)"};
  var calColors = {cal1:"#2563eb",cal2:"#059669",cal3:"#0891b2",cal4:"#7c3aed",cal5:"#c2410c",cal6:"#475569"};

  var rateRows = ["cal1","cal2","cal3","cal4","cal5","cal6"].map(function(ck) {
    var rv = calRatesData[ck] !== undefined ? calRatesData[ck] : "-";
    return '<tr>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;font-weight:500">' + calLabels[ck] + '</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:center;font-weight:800;color:' + calColors[ck] + ';font-size:1.05rem">' + rv + '%</td>'
      + '</tr>';
  }).join("");

  var catGroups = [];
  var currentCat = null;
  workItemsData.forEach(function(item){
    var catName = item.cat || "기타공사";
    if (!currentCat || currentCat.cat !== catName) {
      currentCat = { cat: catName, count: 1, items: [item] };
      catGroups.push(currentCat);
    } else {
      currentCat.count++;
      currentCat.items.push(item);
    }
  });

  var totalCD = 0;
  var workRows = "";
  catGroups.forEach(function(group){
    var groupSubCD = 0;
    group.items.forEach(function(item, itemIdx){
      var cd = Number(item.cd) || 0;
      if (!item.excludeFromSum) {
        groupSubCD += cd;
        totalCD += cd;
      }
      var mo = (cd / 30.5).toFixed(1);
      workRows += '<tr' + (item.excludeFromSum ? ' style="background:#f8fafc"' : '') + '>';
      if (itemIdx === 0) {
        workRows += '<td rowspan="' + group.count + '" style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;font-weight:700;background:#f8fafc;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center">' + escHtml(group.cat) + '</td>';
      }
      workRows += '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;font-weight:500">' + escHtml(item.subCat || item.name || "") + '</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:center;font-size:0.8rem">' + (item.floors || "") + '</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-size:0.8rem">' + (item.unitWD !== undefined && item.unitWD !== "" ? item.unitWD + "일" : "") + '</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#d97706">' + item.wd + ' 일</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#2563eb">' + (item.rate ? item.rate + "%" : "-") + '</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:800;color:' + (cd < 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#2563eb') + '">' + cd + ' 일' + (item.excludeFromSum ? '<br><span style="font-size:7pt;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:600">(산입 제외)</span>' : '') + '</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#7c3aed">' + mo + ' 개월</td>'
        + '<td style="padding:7px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;font-size:0.75rem;color:#475569">' + escHtml(item.note || "") + '</td>'
        + '</tr>';
    });
    workRows += '<tr style="background:#f1f5f9;font-weight:700">'
      + '<td colspan="6" style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;color:#475569">[' + escHtml(group.cat) + '] 소계</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;color:#1e40af;font-size:9pt">' + groupSubCD + ' 일</td>'
      + '<td style="padding:6px 10px;border:1px solid #cbd5e1;text-align:right;color:#5b21b6;font-size:9pt">' + (groupSubCD / 30.5).toFixed(1) + ' 개월</td>'
      + '<td style="border:1px solid #cbd5e1"></td>'
      + '</tr>';
  });

  var totalMonth = (totalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1);

  var html = '<!DOCTYPE html><html lang="ko"><head>'
    + '<meta charset="UTF-8">'
    + '<title>' + pn + ' — 공종별 개략 공사기간 산출서</title>'
    + '<style>'
    + '@import url("https://cdn.jsdelivr.net/gh/orioncactus/pretendard/dist/web/static/pretendard.css");'
    + '* { box-sizing:border-box; margin:0; padding:0; }'
    + 'body { font-family:"Pretendard","Apple SD Gothic Neo",sans-serif; font-size:10pt; color:#0f172a; background:#fff; }'
    + '.page { width:210mm; min-height:297mm; margin:0 auto; padding:16mm 18mm 14mm; }'
    + '.doc-badge { display:inline-block; background:#dbeafe; color:#1e40af; border-radius:20px; padding:3px 12px; font-size:7.5pt; font-weight:700; margin-bottom:8px; }'
    + '.doc-title { font-size:15pt; font-weight:800; color:#0f172a; letter-spacing:-0.02em; margin-bottom:4px; }'
    + '.doc-sub   { font-size:8.5pt; color:#64748b; margin-bottom:0; }'
    + '.doc-meta  { font-size:8pt; color:#64748b; text-align:right; }'
    + '.title-row { display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:flex-end; border-bottom:2.5px solid #0f172a; padding-bottom:12px; margin-bottom:18px; }'
    + '.section-title { font-size:9.5pt; font-weight:700; color:#0f172a; background:#f1f5f9; padding:6px 10px; border-left:3px solid #3b82f6; margin-bottom:10px; margin-top:18px; }'
    + '.info-grid { display:grid; grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr 1fr; gap:8px; margin-bottom:4px; }'
    + '.info-cell { border:1px solid #e2e8f0; border-radius:5px; padding:8px 10px; }'
    + '.info-label { font-size:7pt; color:#64748b; font-weight:600; margin-bottom:3px; }'
    + '.info-value { font-size:9.5pt; font-weight:700; color:#0f172a; }'
    + 'table { width:100%; border-collapse:collapse; font-size:8.5pt; }'
    + 'th { background:#f8fafc; padding:8px 10px; font-weight:700; border:1px solid #cbd5e1; text-align:center; white-space:nowrap; }'
    + 'tbody tr:nth-child(even) td { background:#fafafa; }'
    + 'tfoot td { background:#f1f5f9; font-weight:800; border:1px solid #cbd5e1; }'
    + '.total-row td { padding:9px 10px; font-size:9.5pt; }'
    + '.footer-note { margin-top:20px; border-top:1px solid #e2e8f0; padding-top:10px; font-size:7pt; color:#94a3b8; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; }'
    + '@media print {'
    + '  body { -webkit-print-color-adjust:exact; print-color-adjust:exact; }'
    + '  .page { padding:10mm 14mm; }'
    + '  @page { size:A4 portrait; margin:0; }'
    + '}'
    + '</style></head><body>'
    + '<div class="page">'
    + '<div class="title-row">'
    + '<div>'
    + '<div class="doc-badge">2026 국토부 적정공사기간 가이드라인 기준</div>'
    + '<div class="doc-title">공종별 개략 공사기간 산출서</div>'
    + '<div class="doc-sub">[' + pn + ']&nbsp;&nbsp;시공공정계획 검토</div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="doc-meta">작성일: ' + today + '<br>적용지점: ' + rn + '<br>휴무조건: ' + wtLabel + '</div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="section-title">■ 프로젝트 기본 정보</div>'
    + '<div class="info-grid">'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">사업명</div><div class="info-value">' + pn + '</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">대지면적</div><div class="info-value">' + sa + ' ㎡</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">연면적</div><div class="info-value">' + ta + ' ㎡</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">동 수</div><div class="info-value">' + blkv + ' 개동</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">세대수</div><div class="info-value">' + uv + ' 세대</div></div>'
    + '<div class="info-cell"><div class="info-label">규모</div><div class="info-value">지하 B' + bsv + 'F / 지상 ' + grv + 'F</div></div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<div class="section-title">■ 캘린더별 연간 공사가동률 요약</div>'
    + '<table>'
    + '<thead><tr><th style="text-align:left">공종 구분</th><th>연간 가동률</th></tr></thead>'
    + '<tbody>' + rateRows + '</tbody>'
    + '</table>'
    + '<div class="section-title">■ 공종별 개략 공사기간 산출 (가동률 적용)</div>'
    + '<table>'
    + '<thead><tr>'
    + '<th colspan="2" style="background:#f1f5f9">구 분</th>'
    + '<th rowspan="2" style="width:36px">층수</th>'
    + '<th colspan="5" style="background:#e0f2fe;color:#0369a1">공기산정 시트</th>'
    + '<th rowspan="2">비고</th>'
    + '</tr><tr>'
    + '<th style="background:#f8fafc">대공종</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#f8fafc">소공종</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#fefce8;color:#854d0e">단위WD</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#fefce8;color:#854d0e">순작업일수(W.D)</th>'
    + '<th>가동률</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#eff6ff;color:#1e40af">달력일수(C.D)</th>'
    + '<th style="background:#f5f3ff;color:#5b21b6">산입공기</th>'
    + '</tr></thead>'
    + '<tbody>' + workRows + '</tbody>'
    + '<tfoot><tr class="total-row">'
    + '<td colspan="6" style="text-align:center;padding:9px 10px">🏆&nbsp; 전체 공사기간 산출 최종 합계</td>'
    + '<td style="text-align:right;color:#f59e0b;font-size:11pt;padding:9px 10px">' + totalCD + ' 일</td>'
    + '<td style="text-align:right;color:#f59e0b;font-size:11pt;padding:9px 10px">약 ' + totalMonth + ' 개월</td>'
    + '<td></td>'
    + '</tr></tfoot>'
    + '</table>'
    + '<div class="footer-note">'
    + '<span>※ 본 산출서는 2026년 국토교통부 적정 공사기간 확보를 위한 가이드라인 기상 비작업 기준을 적용하여 산정한 것입니다.</span>'
    + '<span>작성: 공사가동률 산출 시스템</span>'
    + '</div>'
    + '</div>'
    + '<script>window.onload=function(){window.print();}<\/script>'
    + '</body></html>';

  var w = window.open("", "_blank", "width=900,height=1100");

  w.document.open();
  w.document.write(html);
  w.document.close();
}

var editingRecordId = null;

function setVal(id, val) {
  var el = document.getElementById(id);
  if (el) el.value = val;
}

function switchTab(tabKey) {
  document.querySelectorAll(".m-tab").forEach(function(x){ x.classList.remove("active"); });
  document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(function(x){ x.classList.remove("active"); });
  var t = document.querySelector('.m-tab[data-tab="' + tabKey + '"]');
  if (t) t.classList.add("active");
  var targetTab = document.getElementById("tab-" + tabKey);
  if (targetTab) targetTab.classList.add("active");
  aTab = tabKey;
}

function saveResult() {
  var pn = getVal("proj_name", "미입력");
  var regionName = (cWD[reg] || WD["108"]).name;
  var rates = getCalRates();
  var totalCD = 0;
  var itemsSnap = workItems.map(function(item) {
    var r = rates[item.cal] || 1.0;
    var cd = item.customCD !== undefined ? item.customCD : (item.wd > 0 ? Math.ceil(item.wd / r) : 0);
    if (!item.excludeFromSum) {
      totalCD += cd;
    }
    return {
      cat: item.cat || "",
      subCat: item.subCat || item.name || "",
      floors: item.floors !== undefined ? item.floors : "",
      unitWD: item.unitWD !== undefined ? item.unitWD : "",
      wd: item.wd,
      cal: item.cal,
      calName: (CAL_OPTS.find(function(o){return o.v===item.cal;})||{t:"전공종"}).t,
      cd: cd,
      rate: (r * 100).toFixed(1),
      note: item.note || "",
      excludeFromSum: !!item.excludeFromSum
    };
  });
  var calRatesSummary = {};
  ["cal1","cal2","cal3","cal4","cal5","cal6"].forEach(function(ck){
    calRatesSummary[ck] = (rates[ck] * 100).toFixed(1);
  });

  var list = getHistoryList();

  if (editingRecordId) {
    var recIdx = list.findIndex(function(r){ return r.id === editingRecordId; });
    if (recIdx !== -1) {
      list[recIdx].savedAt = new Date().toLocaleString("ko-KR") + " (수정됨)";
      list[recIdx].projName = pn;
      list[recIdx].region = regionName;
      list[recIdx].workType = wt === "6day" ? "주 6일 근무" : "주 5일 근무";
      list[recIdx].projInfo = {
        siteArea: getVal("site_area",""),
        totArea: getVal("tot_area",""),
        blk: getVal("blk",""),
        units: getVal("units",""),
        bsmt: getVal("bsmt",""),
        grnd: getVal("grnd","")
      };
      list[recIdx].calRates = calRatesSummary;
      list[recIdx].workItems = itemsSnap;
      list[recIdx].totalCD = totalCD;
      list[recIdx].totalMonth = (totalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1);
    }
  } else {
    var record = {
      id: Date.now(),
      savedAt: new Date().toLocaleString("ko-KR"),
      projName: pn,
      region: regionName,
      workType: wt === "6day" ? "주 6일 근무" : "주 5일 근무",
      projInfo: {
        siteArea: getVal("site_area",""),
        totArea: getVal("tot_area",""),
        blk: getVal("blk",""),
        units: getVal("units",""),
        bsmt: getVal("bsmt",""),
        grnd: getVal("grnd","")
      },
      calRates: calRatesSummary,
      workItems: itemsSnap,
      totalCD: totalCD,
      totalMonth: (totalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1)
    };
    list.unshift(record);
  }

  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)); } catch(e) {}
  loadHistoryList();

  var btn = document.getElementById("saveBtn");
  var orig = btn.innerHTML;
  btn.innerHTML = editingRecordId ? "✅ 최신 내역으로 수정 완료!" : "✅ 저장 완료!";
  btn.style.background = "linear-gradient(135deg,#10b981,#059669)";
  setTimeout(function(){
    btn.innerHTML = orig;
    btn.style.background = "";
  }, 2000);
}

function loadHistoryRecord(id) {
  var list = getHistoryList();
  var rec = list.find(function(r){ return r.id === id; });
  if (!rec) { alert("해당 내역을 찾을 수 없습니다."); return; }

  editingRecordId = rec.id;

  if (rec.projName) setVal("proj_name", rec.projName);
  if (rec.projInfo) {
    setVal("site_area", rec.projInfo.siteArea || "");
    setVal("tot_area", rec.projInfo.totArea || "");
    setVal("blk", rec.projInfo.blk || "");
    setVal("units", rec.projInfo.units || "");
    setVal("bsmt", rec.projInfo.bsmt || "");
    setVal("grnd", rec.projInfo.grnd || "");
  }

  if (rec.workItems && rec.workItems.length > 0) {
    workItems = JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(rec.workItems));
  }

  var modal = document.getElementById("historyModal");
  if (modal) modal.classList.remove("open");

  switchTab("bal");
  renderWorkTable();

  alert("['" + rec.projName + "'] 산출 내역을 불러왔습니다.\n수정 후 '💾 현재 산출 저장' 버튼을 누르면 기존 기록이 최신 내용으로 최신화(덮어쓰기) 됩니다.");
}

function getHistoryList() {
  try { return JSON.parse(localStorage.getItem(STORAGE_KEY) || "[]"); } catch(e) { return []; }
}

function loadHistoryList() {
  var list = getHistoryList();
  var badge = document.getElementById("historyBadge");
  var grid = document.getElementById("historyGrid");
  if (!grid) return;
  if (badge) badge.textContent = list.length;
  if (list.length === 0) {
    grid.innerHTML = '<div class="history-empty"><div class="history-empty-ico">🗂️</div><div>저장된 산출 내역이 없습니다.<br>💾 <strong>현재 산출 저장</strong> 버튼을 눌러 현재 산출 결과를 기록하세요.</div></div>';
    return;
  }
  var rateColors = {cal1:"#2563eb",cal2:"#059669",cal3:"#0891b2",cal4:"#7c3aed",cal5:"#c2410c",cal6:"#475569"};
  var html = list.map(function(rec) {
    return [
      '<div class="hcard" onclick="viewHistory(' + rec.id + ')" id="hcard-' + rec.id + '">',
      '  <div class="hcard-top">',
      '    <div class="hcard-name">🏗️ ' + escHtml(rec.projName) + '</div>',
      '    <button class="hcard-del" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteHistory(' + rec.id + ')" title="삭제">🗑 삭제</button>',
      '  </div>',
      '  <div class="hcard-time">🕐 ' + escHtml(rec.savedAt) + ' &nbsp;|&nbsp; 📍 ' + escHtml(rec.region) + '</div>',
      '  <div class="hcard-stats">',
      '    <div class="hcard-stat"><div class="hcard-stat-v" style="color:#f59e0b">' + rec.totalCD + '일</div><div class="hcard-stat-l">전체 공사기간</div></div>',
      '    <div class="hcard-stat"><div class="hcard-stat-v" style="color:#059669">' + rec.totalMonth + '개월</div><div class="hcard-stat-l">소요 개월</div></div>',
      '    <div class="hcard-stat"><div class="hcard-stat-v" style="color:#2563eb">' + (rec.calRates && rec.calRates.cal3 ? rec.calRates.cal3 : '-') + '%</div><div class="hcard-stat-l">골조 가동률</div></div>',
      '  </div>',
      '<div class="hcard-btn-row">',
      '  <button class="hcard-view-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();viewHistory(' + rec.id + ')">🔍 상세</button>',
      '  <button class="hcard-edit-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();loadHistoryRecord(' + rec.id + ')">✏️ 수정</button>',
      '  <button class="hcard-pdf-btn" onclick="event.stopPropagation();exportPDF(' + rec.id + ')">📄 PDF</button>',
      '</div>',
      '</div>'
    ].join("");
  }).join("");
  grid.innerHTML = html;
}

function viewHistory(id) {
  var list = getHistoryList();
  var rec = list.find(function(r){ return r.id === id; });
  if (!rec) return;
  var modal = document.getElementById("historyModal");
  var title = document.getElementById("modalTitle");
  var body  = document.getElementById("modalBody");
  title.textContent = "📋 " + rec.projName + " — 산출 내역 상세";

  var calLabels = {cal1:"Cal 1. 토목공사",cal2:"Cal 2. 지하/골조(습식)",cal3:"Cal 3. 지상골조/외부(습식)",cal4:"Cal 4. 내부마감(습식)",cal5:"Cal 5. 외부(건식)",cal6:"Cal 6. 내부마감(건식)"};
  var rateColors = {cal1:"#2563eb",cal2:"#059669",cal3:"#0891b2",cal4:"#7c3aed",cal5:"#c2410c",cal6:"#475569"};

  var infoHtml = [
    '<div class="modal-info-grid">',
    mkInfoCell("사업명", rec.projName),
    mkInfoCell("적용 지역", rec.region),
    mkInfoCell("휴무 조건", rec.workType),
    mkInfoCell("대지면적", rec.projInfo.siteArea ? rec.projInfo.siteArea + " ㎡" : "-"),
    mkInfoCell("연면적", rec.projInfo.totArea ? rec.projInfo.totArea + " ㎡" : "-"),
    mkInfoCell("동수 / 세대수", (rec.projInfo.blk||'-') + "개동 / " + (rec.projInfo.units||'-') + "세대"),
    mkInfoCell("지하층수", rec.projInfo.bsmt ? "B" + rec.projInfo.bsmt + "F" : "-"),
    mkInfoCell("지상층수", rec.projInfo.grnd ? rec.projInfo.grnd + "F" : "-"),
    mkInfoCell("저장 일시", rec.savedAt),
    '</div>'
  ].join("");

  var rates = rec.calRates || {};
  var ratesHtml = '<div class="modal-rates-grid">' +
    Object.keys(calLabels).map(function(ck){
      var v = rates[ck] || "-";
      return '<div class="modal-rate-card"><div class="modal-rate-v" style="color:' + rateColors[ck] + '">' + v + '%</div><div class="modal-rate-l">' + calLabels[ck] + '</div></div>';
    }).join("") + '</div>';

  var items = rec.workItems || [];
  var catGroups = [];
  var currentCat = null;
  items.forEach(function(item){
    var catName = item.cat || "기타공사";
    if (!currentCat || currentCat.cat !== catName) {
      currentCat = { cat: catName, count: 1, items: [item] };
      catGroups.push(currentCat);
    } else {
      currentCat.count++;
      currentCat.items.push(item);
    }
  });

  var workRows = "";
  var modalTotalCD = 0;
  catGroups.forEach(function(group){
    var groupSubCD = 0;
    group.items.forEach(function(item, itemIdx){
      var cd = Number(item.cd) || 0;
      if (!item.excludeFromSum) {
        groupSubCD += cd;
        modalTotalCD += cd;
      }
      var mo = (cd / 30.5).toFixed(1);
      workRows += '<tr' + (item.excludeFromSum ? ' style="background:#f8fafc"' : '') + '>';
      if (itemIdx === 0) {
        workRows += '<td rowspan="' + group.count + '" style="font-weight:700;background:#f8fafc;vertical-align:middle;text-align:center">' + escHtml(group.cat) + '</td>';
      }
      workRows += '<td>' + escHtml(item.subCat || item.name || "") + '</td>' +
        '<td style="text-align:center;font-size:0.78rem">' + (item.floors || "") + '</td>' +
        '<td style="text-align:right;font-size:0.78rem">' + (item.unitWD !== undefined && item.unitWD !== "" ? item.unitWD + "일" : "") + '</td>' +
        '<td style="text-align:right;color:#d97706;font-weight:700">' + item.wd + ' 일</td>' +
        '<td style="text-align:right;font-weight:700;color:#2563eb">' + (item.rate ? item.rate + "%" : "-") + '</td>' +
        '<td style="text-align:right;font-weight:800;color:' + (cd < 0 ? '#dc2626' : '#2563eb') + '">' + cd + ' 일' + (item.excludeFromSum ? '<br><span style="font-size:0.68rem;color:#94a3b8;font-weight:600">(산입 제외)</span>' : '') + '</td>' +
        '<td style="text-align:right;color:#7c3aed;font-weight:700">' + mo + ' 개월</td>' +
        '<td style="font-size:0.75rem;color:#475569">' + escHtml(item.note || "") + '</td>' +
        '</tr>';
    });
    workRows += '<tr style="background:#f1f5f9;font-weight:700">' +
      '<td colspan="6" style="text-align:right;color:#475569">[' + escHtml(group.cat) + '] 소계</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:right;color:#1e40af;font-size:0.88rem">' + groupSubCD + ' 일</td>' +
      '<td style="text-align:right;color:#5b21b6;font-size:0.88rem">' + (groupSubCD / 30.5).toFixed(1) + ' 개월</td>' +
      '<td></td></tr>';
  });

  var workTbl = [
    '<div class="tbl-wrap"><table class="modal-work-tbl">',
    '<thead><tr>',
    '<th colspan="2" style="background:#f1f5f9">구 분</th><th rowspan="2" style="width:36px">층수</th><th colspan="5" style="background:#e0f2fe;color:#0369a1">공기산정 시트</th><th rowspan="2">비고</th>',
    '</tr><tr>',
    '<th style="background:#f8fafc">대공종</th><th style="background:#f8fafc">소공종</th><th>단위WD</th><th>순작업일수</th><th>가동률</th><th>달력일수</th><th>산입공기</th>',
    '</tr></thead>',
    '<tbody>' + workRows + '</tbody>',
    '<tfoot><tr>',
    '<td colspan="6" style="text-align:center">🏆 전체 공사기간 산출 최종 합계</td>',
    '<td style="text-align:right;color:#f59e0b;font-size:1rem">' + (rec.totalCD || modalTotalCD) + ' 일</td>',
    '<td style="text-align:right;color:#f59e0b;font-size:1rem">약 ' + (rec.totalMonth || (modalTotalCD / 30.5).toFixed(1)) + ' 개월</td>',
    '<td></td>',
    '</tr></tfoot>',
    '</table></div>'
  ].join("");

  var btnRow = '<div style="margin-top:24px;padding-top:16px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;justify-content:flex-end;gap:10px">' +
    '<button class="hcard-edit-btn" onclick="loadHistoryRecord(' + rec.id + ')" style="padding:8px 16px;font-size:0.85rem">✏️ 불러오기 및 수정</button>' +
    '<button class="hcard-pdf-btn" onclick="exportPDF(' + rec.id + ')" style="padding:8px 16px;font-size:0.85rem">📄 PDF 출력</button>' +
    '<button class="hcard-del" onclick="event.stopPropagation();deleteHistory(' + rec.id + ');document.getElementById(\'historyModal\').classList.remove(\'open\')" style="padding:8px 16px;font-size:0.85rem">🗑 삭제</button>' +
    '<button class="hcard-view-btn" onclick="document.getElementById(\'historyModal\').classList.remove(\'open\')" style="padding:8px 16px;font-size:0.85rem">닫기</button>' +
    '</div>';

  body.innerHTML =
    '<div class="modal-section-title">📌 프로젝트 기본 정보</div>' + infoHtml +
    '<div class="modal-section-title">📊 캘린더별 연간 가동률</div>' + ratesHtml +
    '<div class="modal-section-title">🏗️ 공종별 공사기간 산출 내역</div>' + workTbl +
    btnRow;

  modal.classList.add("open");
}

function mkInfoCell(label, value) {
  return '<div class="modal-info-cell"><div class="modal-info-label">' + escHtml(label) + '</div><div class="modal-info-value">' + escHtml(String(value||'-')) + '</div></div>';
}

function deleteHistory(id) {
  if (!confirm("이 산출 내역을 삭제하시겠습니까?")) return;
  var list = getHistoryList().filter(function(r){ return r.id !== id; });
  try { localStorage.setItem(STORAGE_KEY, JSON.stringify(list)); } catch(e){}
  loadHistoryList();
}

function escHtml(s) {
  return String(s)
    .replace(/&/g,"&amp;").replace(/</g,"&lt;").replace(/>/g,"&gt;")
    .replace(/"/g,"&quot;").replace(/'/g,"&#39;");
}

// ─── Init ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
document.addEventListener("DOMContentLoaded",function(){
  if(typeof initRegionSelect === "function") initRegionSelect();
  document.getElementById("regionSelect").addEventListener("change",function(e){reg=e.target.value;calcAndRender();});
  document.getElementsByName("wt").forEach(function(r2){r2.addEventListener("change",function(e){wt=e.target.value;cHol=wt==="6day"?H6.slice():H5.slice();calcAndRender();});});
  document.querySelectorAll(".c-tab").forEach(function(b){b.addEventListener("click",function(){document.querySelectorAll(".c-tab").forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove("active");});b.classList.add("active");cal=b.dataset.cal;renderOpTable();renderChart();});});
  document.querySelectorAll(".m-tab").forEach(function(t2){t2.addEventListener("click",function(){document.querySelectorAll(".m-tab").forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove("active");});t2.classList.add("active");aTab=t2.dataset.tab;document.querySelectorAll(".tab").forEach(function(x){x.classList.remove("active");});document.getElementById("tab-"+aTab).classList.add("active");if(aTab==="bal")renderWorkTable();else if(aTab==="rpt")renderRpt();});});
  ["proj_name","site_area","tot_area","blk","units","bsmt","grnd"].forEach(function(id){var e=document.getElementById(id);if(e)e.addEventListener("input",function(){if(aTab==="rpt")renderRpt();});});
  document.getElementById("saveBtn").addEventListener("click",saveResult);
  // 모달 닫기
  document.getElementById("modalClose").addEventListener("click",function(){
    document.getElementById("historyModal").classList.remove("open");
  });
  document.getElementById("historyModal").addEventListener("click",function(e){
    if(e.target===this) this.classList.remove("open");
  });
  document.addEventListener("keydown",function(e){
    if(e.key==="Escape") document.getElementById("historyModal").classList.remove("open");
  });
  loadHistoryList();
  calcAndRender();
});
</script>
</body>
</html>
